In the flagellar motor, FliM is part of the C-ring (switch complex). Proteins that interact with FliM include:
1. CheY (response regulator)

3. FliN (motor protein)
4. Mot proteins

For CheY-FliM interaction specifically, the interaction surface has been mapped to specific regions. But there&#x27;s also a concept of a &quot;motif&quot; - a short recurring sequence pattern.

A covalent intermediate is formed when the enzyme forms a temporary covalent bond with the substrate during catalysis. This is characteristic of certain enzyme mechanisms:
